For IoT campaigns, the supported types are `enable_profile`, `disable_profile`, and `delete_profile`.nnPayload Structuren- `accountId` (string, required) The unique identifier for the account under which the campaign will be created.n- `type` (string, required) Specifies the type of the campaign. Valid types include `steering`, `download_profile`, `disable_profile`, `delete_profile`, `audit_esim`, `add_imsi`, `lock_imei`, `fallback_profile_mgmt` and `enable_profile`.n- `filters` (object, 8601 format.nnDepending on the `type` of the campaign, additional properties may be requiredn- `steering` Must include `steeringList` specifying the list to be used.n- `download_profile` Requires a
